Da ka jingmynjur jong ka Synod kaba la long ha 
ka Balang Kyrdem, Ri Bhoi Presbytery ha u March, 
15-1S\ 1978, ka Sunday School Committee ka la 
pynshet halor jong nga ia kane ka jingkit kaba khia

ban pynkhreh ia ka Kommentari ka Gospel kat- 
kum u Markos na ka bynta ka jingpulc ha ki Skul 
Pynpaw hapoh ka Synod ha ka snem 1980-81. Ha, 
kaba pynkhreh ia kane ka Kommentari, nga la ioh 
jingmyntoi kaba khraw lyngba ki kot kiba nga la 
hap ban wad jingiarap ia kiba ngam da donkam ban 
da jer kyrteng la ki hangne. 


Kane ka long tang ka jingpyrshang kaba rit eh 
jong nga ban larap lem ia ki Skul Pynpaw jong ka 
Synod jong ngi. Nga kyrpad eh ia ki nongpule haba 
ki shem ia k;no kino ki jingduna ha kane ka kot 
namar ki jingbymshai bha ha ka jingbatai ia ki bynta 
kiba kham donkam, ba kin map ia nga. Nga kyrpad 
ia ki nongpule ruh ba kin ym shaniah eh tang ha 
kane ka Kommentari ; hynrei ka Kommentari kaba 
lah ban ai jingshai kaba bha tarn ban ia kaba u briew 
u thoh ne batai, ka long ka jingpule da kaba duwai 
bad puson ha la ka dohnud bad wad ruh ia ka bor 
jong ka jingpynshai U Mynsiem Bakhuid.

Ka Jinglamphrang 

Ko jingsdong buh iingthoh ia ka Gospel 

Ka ktien 'Gospel* ka mut ka Khubor babha ne ka khu- 
bor ba dap da ka |ingkmen kaba wan hiar na U Blei sha ki 
briew. Ka long ka Khubor ba ai jingkmen bad ai jing* 
kyrmen ia kl briew bad kata ka long lyngba U Jisu Khrlst, 
U Khun U Biel, U Nongpynim bad U Nongslewspah ia ka 
pop ka pyrthei. 

Ka Gospel shaphang ka jingim bad ka jingtrei U Jisu 
Khrlst, ka wan mih paw hadlen ba U Trai Jisu urn don 
shuh ha kane ka pyrthei kum u briew. Katba ngi lah ban 
loh jingeip, ba ha shuwa katei ka por, ym don ki jingthoh 
Jingtar k!ba biang ban pynshisha ba ia don ki jingthoh ki 
ba iathuh bniah shaphang ka jingim bad ka jingtrei U jisu 
Khrist, Hynrei ki jinglathuhkhana pateng la pateng sha- 
phang long u, ki long da ki jingiathuh ktien da ki briew 
hi kiba la im ha ka por jong u bad ka hiar pateng la pa- 
teng. Dei ki Khrlstan kiba nyngkong hi kiba la pynsah 
ha ki jinglathuhkhana pateng shaphang ka jingim bad ka 
jingtrei jong u bad ia nang pynshisha pat da kito ki briew 
ki bym dei kl khristan kiba la im ha kito ki por kiba la 
buh jingthoh ruh katto katne shaphang jong u, 

Ka daw ba kongsan eh ba kito ki Khristan banyng- 
kong kl la buh jingkynmaw kyndong shaphang ka Jingim 
bad ka jingtrei jong u, ka long ba ki ia shem bad la mad 
ba U jisu Khrlst urn long tang ka dur jong uta u ba la iap 
ia siem; hynrei u long uta uba iai im ryngkat bad kita 
kiba ngeit ha u ha ki por baroh. Ki ngeit skhem ruh ba u 
long uba lah ban iasyllok bad ia ki briew ha man la ki jing^ 
jia jong ka jingim jong ki. Ki ngeit skhem ba u long u 
Khun U Blei uba khlem pop bad ba kl ktien jong u ki

2 


long kiba shisha. Ki ioh-i ruh ba ha u bad l/ngba jong u 
ka don ka lad ka jingpynim ia ki bynriew baroh. Wat lada 
um don shuh ha ka pyrthei kum u briew ; hynrei kum U 
Biel pat, u iai im bad iai pynpaw ha la ki nongbud jong u 
ha ki rukom kiba pher ba pher. 

Haba ngi pule ia ka jingim bad jingtrei jong u, ngim 
don jingartaiien satia ba ki synran jong u hi ki iachuh bad 
phla shaphang jong u ha kl briew baroh ; ia ha kaba rich 
no kaba paw. Suki suki ki nongthoh ki la mih ban thoh 
ia ki Gospel. La ong ba u Papias u Bishop ki Hieropoiis 
uba la im kumbaha ka snem 140 H. K., u la thoh kumne : 
‘"u Markos, da shlsha uba long u nongbataiktien ia u Petros, 
u la thoh shai katba u lah ban kynmaw ia kiei kiei baroh 
kiba U Trai u la kren bad u la leh^ wat la kam da long 
ryntih bha ruh. La um shy n li lohsngew da lade lymne 
long u synran uba ha krung U Trai : hynrei kumba la la 
thuh, u la bud synrop synrop la u Petros. U la ju shim ia ki 
jingiathuh u Petros, ia ki ktien U Trai Jisu hi u kren bad u 
pyndonkam ha kl jingdawa jong kl por. Ki jingiathuh u 
Petros ki long ban lehnoh ia kiei kiei kiba u la loh sngew 
bad ia kiei kiei kiba U Trai u la leh khnang ban pynrung 
ia kine ki ktien bashisha ha kito kiba sngap ia u/' 

Ngi loh jingtip ruh ba u Justin Martyr, uba laim kumba 
ha ka snem 161 H. K., u la iathuh biu Maricos u la thoh 
ia ka Gospel katkum ki ktien ba u la iohsngew na u 
Petros. Kumjuh ruh U Ireneus, ubi la Im kumba ha ka 
snem 180 H, K , u la Iathuh ba dei u Markos ubi long

u nongbatal ktien u Petros uba la thoh ia ki jingialap u 
Petros shaphang U jisu ha ki briew kiba ha kito kl por. 
Kum u nongbatai ktien ia u Petros, bunslen u pyndonkam 
ia ki ktien Aramaik, kaba long ka ktien ba U jisu u shait 
kren bad ba la pyndonkam ha kito ki por. Kumta ha kl por 
ki por, haba u Petros u kren, u ju lohsngew la kl juh ki 
ktien kiba U Jisu hi u kren. ‘‘Talltha kum” (5:41); 
‘Ephphatha* (7 : 34) ; ^Korban' (7:11); -Abba' (14 : 36) 
& ‘Eloi Elol Lama sabakhthani’ (15 : 34). Kane ka pynshi- 
sha ba ka Gospel kat kum u Markos ka wan lyngba kl kyn- 
tien bad jingkren u Petros bad ka long kaba nyngkongeh 
na ki Gospel baroh ba la thoh shaphang U Jisu Khrist.

II Ki Synoptic Gospel 

La ju khot ia ki lai tylli kl Gospel, kata Mathaios. 
Markos bad Lukas- **ki Synoptic Gospels*’ (Sinoptik Gos- 
pel). Kane ka del ka ktlen Grik kaba mut *Ki Gospel kiba 
del ban pulesynrop synrop* Lada ngi pule ia ki ngin shem 
ba don tci jln^JIa bad kl kyntien kiba la long mar kumjuh 
kumjuh. Kum ki nuksa ; Ka jingbsa la ki san hajar (Mar- 
kos 6:30-44; Mathaios 14: 12-21 bad Lukas 9; 10-17). 
Ka lingpynkoit la uba pang iap-shiliang (Markos 2: 1-12; 
Mathaios 9: 1-8 bad Lukas 5: 17-26) bad kiwei kiwei de. 

la ka Gospel katkum u Markos la pyniabynta ha ki 
105 tylll ki bynta. Na kine 93 tylll ki bynta lah bin shem 
ha kl jingthoh u Mathaios bad 8i tylli ki bynta lah ban 
shem ha u Lukas. Katkum u Markos ngi shem ba don tang 
661 tylli ki dkhot. ha u Mathaios don 1.068 bad ha u

Lukas pat don haduh 1,449 tylli ki dkhot, Haba ngi btshar 
bniah ia kine ngi shem ba u Mathaios u la pyntam ktlen 
haduh 51 na ka shlspah na kaba don ha u Markos kum- 
juh ruh u Lukas u la pyncam ktlen 53 na ka shispah. Na kl 
jingthoh u Mathaios bad u Lukas ngi shem ba don tang 24 
tylli ki dkhot ki bym shym la shem ha ka Gospel katkum u 
Markos. Kumca ngi lah ban shim bi kine arngut'kl nong< 
thoh ki la shim ia ka tynrai na ki Jfngthuh u Markos da 
kaba kl pyrshang ban pyniahap ruh bad ki jingthuh 
Jong ki. 

III. U Moricos, u nongthuh io Ico Gospel 

Ha ka Testament Bathymmal la lathuh shibun sha- 
phang Jong u. La ong ba u long u khun Jong kawei ka 
kynthei Jiw ka briew ka ba-don ba-em kaba ha Jerusalem 
kaba ka kyrteng Jong ka ka long ka Mari. Dei ha ka iing 
Jong ka ba la Ju pynlong ka Jaka ia shem Jong kf Khristan 
kiba nyngkong kaba mut ka Jingiashemlang Jong ka 
balang ha ka por kaba ka dang lung (Kl Kam. 12 : 12). 
Ka Kyrteng Markos ne Markus ka long ka kyrteng Roman 
la kaba la pyndonkam da ki jentil ha kito kl por. Hynrel ka 
kyrteng tynrai Jong u ka long ka kyrteng jlW*loanis. Kumta 
ha kl katto katne ki bynta Jong ka Ktlen U BleL la shem

la kane ka kyrteng ‘loanis Markos.’ La ong ruh ba u long 
u pyrsa jong u Barnabas, u lok jong u Paul ha ka jinglel : 
Missionary kaba nyngkong jong u. Kumta u loanis Mar- 
kos, u la ioh la ka jingpynsan ha ka Iyer ba kynja khrlstan 
lyngba ka kmie bad u kni jong u. Ngi kynmaw ha ka jlng-

ia leit missionary kaba nyngkong jong u Paul bad u Barna- 
bas» ki la shim lem ia u Markos ban long u kynja nongthoh 
ne nongbud lem jong ki (Kl kam. 12:25). Hynrel kane ka 
jinglelt ka la don ka jingjia kaba sngewsih mynba ki la poi 
Perga, u Makkos u la del ban leic phai noh sha la Ving 
(Kl kam. 13 : 13). Kane ka lah ban long ba u Markos u 
sngew sheptieng ia kl jlngma jingmynsaw ha ka lynti laid 
kiba lah ban jia ne lehse kum uba dang kham khynnah ka 
•la lap ka jingkynmaw Ving. Don pat kiba ong ba u la leit

kaba ar pat, u Barnabas u la mut ban lalam blang ia u 
Markos, hynrei u Paul pat um mon shuh ban iaiam lem la u 
(Kl Kam. 15 ; 37-40). Kane ka la pynlong ka jingiakhlad 
lok hapdeng u Paul bad u Bsirnabas. Ki khana pateng ki 
jathuh ba u Markos u la jah katto katne por na ka History. 
Hynrel la iathuh pat, ba u la leit hiar sha ki phang ka Ijipt 
shaduh Aleksandrfa bad u la seng ia ka balang ha Aleksan- 
dria. Hadien ngl shem pat, ba haba u Paul u ia don ha ka 
byndl ha Rome hangta u Markos u ia don lem bad u (Kol. 
4: 10). Ha kawel pat ka shithi kaba u Paul u la thoh, u la 
kdew ba u Markos u la long uwel na kl para ba ia trel lem 
bad u (Philemon, dk. 24) Haba u Paul u thoh sha u Timo- 
thi u da kynthoh ruh da kaba ong *to shim ruh ia u 
Markos bad wallam ia u sha me^ (I Tim. 4 : II). Kane ka 
pynshlsha ba u Paul hi u la iasuk bad u Markos- *suk kiba 
pynlasuk naba yn khot la kl kl khun U BleP (Math. 5:9). La 
ka long kumno kumno ruh ha ka jingiadei jong u bad u 
Paul, u Markos u la ioh ia lade la ka nam ka kyrteng ^u 
briew uba la siewspah da lade’. La ong ruh ba'u briew uba 
u Pa^l u kwah khadduh eb ha shuwa ban lap. u lone 
u Markos. ^ ®

IV. Ko Jingthrru u Markos ho kobo thoh io ko Gospol 

1. U Markos u thoh fa kane ka Gospel sha kito ki 
kynhun Khristan kiba la sdan? ban mad ia ki jingpynshah 
shitom bein bad don ruh wat kiba la shah lap na ka bynta 
ka kyrteng U Jisu Khrist. La ju ong ia kane ka Gospel kac 
kum u Markos 'Ka Gospel na ka bynta ki Martyr.’ Kumta 
ka jingthmu ba kongsan eh ka long ban ai mynslem ia kito 
kiba dang dei ban shah shitom na ka bynta U Trai Jisu, da 
kaba u pynshong nongrim ha kine ki iai tylll ki jingshisha 

(a) Ba U Jlsu u la shah shitom kumjuh kumba ki 
nongbud jong u kl la shah namar la khot ia ki ruh ban la 
shah shitom lem bad U. (13:9-13; Math, 10 : 17-20 ; 
Luk. 12:4) 

(b) Ba U Jisu u la maham ba kito kiba bud ia u ki dei 
ban ia shim bynta bad ki jingshah jong u (8 : 34-35 ; Math 
10 : 28-39 ; 16 : 24-25 ; Luk. 9 : 23 & 24) 

(k) Ba ki jingkular jong u ki long kiba skhem, ba ki 
nongbud kin loh ia ka bainong lada kin ieng skhem ha ka 
jingngelt (13 : 13 ; Math. 24 : 9-13 ; Luk, 21 : 12-19).

pynshlsha ba U jisu u long uta U Messaiah ne U Khrist. U 
sdang fa ka Gospel da kaba ong *kaba sdang ka Gospel U

sha. U la iathuh ruh ba kito kiba la iohsngew bad ion-i la 
u ba ki la lyngngoh ia ka jinghikai jong u (I :,2) *ki a 
sngewkyndit’ (I : 27). Wat kl synran jong u ruh ki la shep-

.../(4 : 41 Math. 8:27; Luk. 8 : 25). Hynrei ngi loh-i pat 
ba U jisu hi u iaieh ban buhrieh ia kane ka jingshisha ba 
u long u Messaiah. Lada ngi pule ngin shem ba u la pyr- 
shang ban pynsngap jar wat ia ki ksuid bad ki briew kiba la 
pyrshang ban kdew ba u long u Messaiah (I ; 34 ; 5 : 43 j.

Khrist n« U Messaiah pynban ong k^ ktien ‘u la hukom

Ka iaka bad ka por ba la thoh ia ka Gospel 

Ka jaka ; Ki don kl {ingtharai kiba pher ba pher ia ka 
jaka ha kaba la thoh ia kane ka Gospel. U Papias bad u 
Ireneus kirn shym la kynthoh ei ei ruh hifigno la thoh

ei jong ki ka shai ba dei ha Rom bi la thoh ia ka.’*Ka 
don sa kawel pit ka jingkynthoh kibi ong ba ia kane ka 
Gospel la thoh ‘ha ki bynta jong ka ri Itall.’ U Clement 
jong ka Alexandria bad u Origen pat kl pynskhem ba la 
thoh ia ka ha Rom. Ki bibun ki nongwat bnlah la ka tynral 
ba la thoh ia kine ka Gospel, ki pynshisha ba la thoh ia 
ka ha Rom. Nimar !<"t kumba ka paw ba ka jingthmu 
jong kane ka Gospel hi ka long sha ka baling kaba kiba 
bun kl dkhot jong ka ki long ki Jentil (7 : ^ ; 1 1 ; 13 ; Math : 
15 : 1-9; 12 : 42) ha kaba li kl jingshahpynshitom beih la 
khmihlynti kming, ni ka bynta ka jingtynjuh ia ka jing- 
ngeit jong ki kaba hi U Jisu Khrist. (8 : 34-48 ; 10 : 38 ; 
13 : 9..I3 ; Math. 24 : 3-14 ; Luk. 21 : 7-19). 

Ka por: Ka jingiithuh jong u Papias bad u Ireneus 
haba shim kyllum ka long ba u Markos u ia thoh la ka 
Gospel hadien kn jingiap u Petros ; namar la lathuh ba u 
Petros u la iap ha ka por jong ka jingpynshitom beift ia 
ki Khrlstan da u patsha Nero ; kata kumba ha ka snem 64 
H.K. bad lehse hadien khyndiat bnai ka la shim por ia u 
Markos ban thoh ia ki, kata kumba ha ka snem 64 H.K. 

La ka long ba ha katel ka snem la thoh ia ka ; hynrei 
la ong ba ka jingpaw pyrthei pat ka long hadien ka (snem 
75 H.K. La mih ruh ki jingpyrshang ban shim la ka nong-

don ka jingiakhih pyrshah jong ki Jlw ia ka bor synshar lah

LYNNONG I 


Ka Komentari la Ka Gospel 
U Markos 

Ka jingiathuh khano shaphang u loanis, u 
Nongpynboptis 

(Dkhot 1-8., Math. 3 1 I 12, Luk. 3:1-18 loan. 1 : 18-28) 

U loanis u long tang u nongrah khubor ia ka jingwan

U Trai Jisu. Ka khubor jong u ka long shaphang ka jingim 
bathymmai, ka jinglong hok long belt jong ki briew ha ka 
iiang ka im lang ka sah Jang bad ba ki briew km pynkhreh 
ia ka jingpdiang ia Uta u Messaiah lane U Khnst u ban sa 
wan shcn. 

Dkhot 1. ^'Kaba sdang Jca Gospel U Jisu Khrist u khun 
If BiciJ' Kane ka thcw ia ka jingiathuh kaba naduh kaba 
sdang ia ka Gospel U Jisu Khrist kaba h^ng hi ruh ka Mis- 
sion jong ka Balang Khristan bad kaba dang iai bteng 
haduh kine ki por (Ki Kain 1:1). Gospel : Ka mut ka 
khubor babha. Jisu \ Ka long ka ktien Grik ‘lesus* ba la 
pynwan na ka kricn Hebru ‘Joshua’ ne ‘Hoshea.^ Ka 
long ka kyrteng briew ba la ju pyndonkam bha ha ka Testa- 
ment Barim. (Deut,34 : 9 ; I Khron. 7 : 20 ; 27 : 7 ; 27 : 20 ; 
2 Syiem 1:1, 6.). Khrist : Ha ka knen Hebru ka mut u 
‘Messiah’ ia uba ki Jiw ki khmih lynti ba un wan sha ka 
pyrthei kum u Nongsynshar Lahduh jong ki ia uba U Blei 
hi u la pynsich ha u da ka bor baroh. Une un pyndem ia 
ka pyrthei baroh ha ka bor jong u. Khm U Blei : U 
Markos u ngeit skhem ba U Jisu Khrist u long da shisha u 
Khun Ij Blei. Kane ka Gospel kaba u la thoh ka thew hi 
ban pynshisha ba U Jisu Khrisc u long u Khun U Blei. 

Dkhot 2 & 3 : (Math. 3:1-18 Luk. 3:1-18): Ki 
long ki kyntien ba la shim kylliang na ki ar tylli ki bynta 
jong ki nongiathuhlypa Malakhi 3 : 1 bad Isaiah 40 : 3. 



2 


“Ha khmih nga phah ia u oongbuhai jong nga hakhmat 
jong me.’ Nongbuhai : kane ka mut u nonglamkhubor ne u

nongmaitlynti, uba dei ban pynkhreh ia ka jingwanu Syiem.

Ka Pharshi u nongbet

Dkhot 1 & 2 : **Ki la poi sha shiliang...,....ka ri ki nong 
Gadara.’’

na Mihngt sha Sepngi sha ka ri jong ki Jentil, ka Deka* 
polis. U Markos bad u Lukas ha ka ktien tynrai ki ja 
thuh ba ka dei ka Gerasene, kacba u Mathaios pat u ong 
ka Gadara. Ka Gadara ka long tang kumba 6 met ne lO 
Km. na ka duriaw Galilaia katba ka Gerasene pat ka long 
kumba 30 mer ne 48 Km. na ka duriaw. U Markos u 
shem ba kane ka jaka ka dei ka shnong kaba khain kong- 
san bad kaba hajan eh ia ka duriaw. Ka long ka jaka 
mawsiang kaba dap daki krem ki kroh kaba la ju pyn* 
donkam kum ka jaka tep briew ha kine ki krem. 

Dkhot 2 & 3 : **Haba u la mih noh da ki kynjri.*'

kyhdit kyndit la wan u briew uba shong ksuid uba shong 
ha kita ki maw jingtep. U briew u la long u bym ia long 
shuh ban ia shong ryngkat iasah ryngkat, u la shong sa 
tang ha ki maw jingtep, ka jaka shong jong ki ksuid 
kumba ngi pyrkhat u long uba khlain ba ka bor ksuid jong 
u bad ym don ba lah ban teh ban khum ia u. La M;an tan 
ia u ; u la wan ban iashem bad U Jisu.

Shaphang ki jingjynthut 

(Dkhot 42-50; Math. 18: 6-9; Luk. 17: 1-2). 

Dkhot 42 : “Jar u ban pynthut ha duriaw.**' 

Ban pynthut ia u para uba tlot ka rnut ban buh ia 
lade ha ka jingpynshitom kaba bymjukut, U Maw tyUiai : 
Don ar jaid ki maw tylliat kew ha ti Palestine. U 
don u maw tylliat uba ki pyniaid da ka kri ia kaba ki 
kynthei Jiw hi ki ju pyndonkam. Don pat u maw tylliat 
uba khraw bad haba tylliat kew dei ban pyniaid ia u da 
u kada. Une u maw tylliat hangne u mut uba dei ban 
ring da u kada. Bred ruh ia u ha duriaw : Ka jaka kaba 
yni lah shuh ban wan phai. Ka shnong ka lawpaw. 

Dkhot 43-48 : Kine ki jingong ha kine ki dkhot ki 
bteng na ka Dkhot 42 : ‘Jar uba pynthut.” Ka long ka 
ba dei eh haduh kacca katra ban ioh rung hapoh ka Hima 
U Blei bad ka kham bha ban leit rung kum uba dkoh, 
uba matlah ban ia kaba duh ia ka. Ka Jingim : Ka mut ka 
Hima U Blei. Ka dujok: Ka ktien tynrai “Gehena” Kaba 
mut ka them jong ka Hinom kaba don shabar na Jeru- 
salem. (2 Syiem 23 : 10 ; Jer. 7 : 31 ; 32 : 35)j ka jaka ban

thang ia ki jakhlia baroh, ka jaka jong ka jingpynshitom. 
U ksain um kip: Ka jingshyrkhei jong ka jingpynshitom 
namar ka jingpynthuc ia kiwei pat. 

Dkhot 49 : “Naba ia u briew ....jingknia baroh.” 

Kane ka long ka jingpynshyrkhei ia ki jingtynjuh ia 
ki bangeic. la ki Khristan yn cynjuh, yn phrang ha ka ding 
bad yn kyllan mluh. Lah ban pynshai kumne : fi) Ka ding 
ka thang luc ia ki jakhlia baroh bad ha ka juh ka por pat 
ka pynkhuid ia ki. Ka mluh ruh ka pynkhuid ia kaei kaei 
bad kam pynsniew ruh ia ka jingbam. (ii) la ki Khristan 
dei ban pynkhuid da ka ding jong ka jingshah shitom 
bad (iii) la ki Khristan dei ban pynbaptis da ka ding u 
Mynsiem bakhuid. 

Dkhot 50 : *'Ka mluh ka bha paralok hi.” 

Pule Mathaios 5 : 13 ; Luk. 14 : 34. Ka mut ba lada ki 
synran ki la duh ia ka mynsiem jong ka jingaiti bad 
jingshakri^ kam don shuh ka jingmyntoi. Lada ki synran 



ki pynduh noh ia ka bor ka Gospel ha ka pyrthei, kata

de. U Jisu u mut ba rang ka jingim kaba la pynkhuid 
phar na ka ma-ladc bad kaba la pyndap pat da U Khrisc 
dei kata ka jingim kaba lah ban tm ha ka jingiasyllok 
bashisha bad ki para briew.

Ka Spah bod ko Boinong 

(Dkhot 23-31 ; Math. 19: 23-30*; Luk. 18 : 21-30) 

Dkhot 25 : “ Haba U Jisu te u la khmih hima U 

Blei.” 

Ki Jiw ki la ju pyrkhat ba ka spah ka long ka jing- 
kyrkhu jong U Blei. Hynrei hangne pat U Jisu u pyn-i 
ia ka jingshyrkhei jong ka spah. kaba long ka jingkhang- 
lad ia u briew hi ban rung shapoh ka hima U Blei, kaba 
ia pyrshah ia ka jingpyrkhat jong ki. Ba don spah : Ka 
ktien spah hangne ka thew ia ki mar ki mata, ki jingdon 
jingem kiba lah ban thaw paisa. 

Dkhot 24-25 : Ha kine ki dkhot la pyn-i ba ki synran ki 
la sngewlyngngoh namar ka long mar pyrshah bad ka rukom 
pyrkhat ki Jiw. Namar ha ki hi, katba u briew u nang ioh 
spah, karra ruh kan nang pynthikna ia u ban rung ha ka 
hima U Blei. Kumta U Jisu u la ban biang sa shisien ia 
kine ki ktien, ba ka long kaba eh haduh katta katta ia kito 
ki briew kiba buh ia la ka jingshaniah tang ha ka spah bad 
lyngba jong ka kin ioh jaka ha ka Hima U Blei. Kito kiba 
shaniah ha ka spah (i) ki buh ia la ki dohfiud ha ka pyr- 
thei bad klet noh ia U Blei, (ii) ki pyndonkam ia ka spah 
wat ban thied ia ka jingim bymjukut. Ka spah hi ka long 
ka nongtuh ia ka jingtm bymjukut lada ki briew kin ai 
lad ba ma ka kan long ka kynrad jong ki. Li Jisu u pyn-i 
shuh shuh ia ka jingeh ia nba riewspah ban rung sha ka 
Hima U Blei. Ka thliew thyrnia : La ju iathuh ba ha ka

nongbah kaba heh, la ju ker da ka kut kaba jrong baroh 
sawdong ia ka bad ka don tang kawei ka jingkhangbah 
kaba ki briew ki lah ban rung na kaba ki pyniaid ia ka 
kha-V ka pateng. Don ruh sa kiwei kiwei pat ki khyrdop
